{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_0145WzQFAJCFKPUFR1fsPRMG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01T1YG5oVQmfUEJY2qfhALzP","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/normalize_defect_reasons.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nManufacturing Test Center Defect Reason Normalization\nNormalizes hand-written defect reasons to standardized codebook entries\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport re\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Tuple\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\n\n\nclass DefectReasonNormalizer:\n    def __init__(self, codebook_dir=\"/app/data\"):\n        self.codebook_dir = codebook_dir\n        self.codebooks = {}\n        self.load_codebooks()\n\n    def load_codebooks(self):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n        products = [\"P1_POWER\", \"P2_CTRL\", \"P3_RF\"]\n        for product in products:\n            codebook_path = f\"{self.codebook_dir}/codebook_{product}.csv\"\n            with open(codebook_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                self.codebooks[product] = list(reader)\n\n    def preprocess_text(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize text for matching - remove extra spaces, normalize case\"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return \"\"\n        # Normalize whitespace\n        text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n        return text.strip()\n\n    def extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> set:\n        \"\"\"Extract keywords from text (both English and Chinese)\"\"\"\n        text_lower = text.lower()\n        # Split by common delimiters\n        keywords = re.split(r'[,，、;；/\\s]+', text_lower)\n        # Filter out very short keywords and common words\n        keywords = {k.strip() for k in keywords if len(k.strip()) > 1}\n        return keywords\n\n    def segment_text(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw defect reason into individual defect descriptions.\n        Handles multiple defects separated by delimiters like &, ; / etc.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return []\n\n        # Common separators: & ; + / 、and newlines\n        # But be careful not to split on / within descriptions like \"open/short\"\n        segments = []\n\n        # First split by strong delimiters\n        # Use pattern to split by & or ; or 、or multiple spaces with specific patterns\n        parts = re.split(r'\\s*[&;、]\\s*|\\s+(?:and|AND)\\s+', text)\n\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if part:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def calculate_keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate keyword-based matching score\"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = self.extract_keywords(segment)\n        codebook_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', ''))\n\n        if not segment_keywords or not codebook_keywords:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Calculate Jaccard similarity\n        intersection = segment_keywords & codebook_keywords\n        union = segment_keywords | codebook_keywords\n\n        if not union:\n            return 0.0\n\n        return len(intersection) / len(union)\n\n    def calculate_fuzzy_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate fuzzy string matching score using standard_label\"\"\"\n        segment_lower = segment.lower()\n        label_lower = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '').lower()\n\n        # Use SequenceMatcher for fuzzy matching\n        return SequenceMatcher(None, segment_lower, label_lower).ratio()\n\n    def calculate_component_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Boost score if component/net names match\"\"\"\n        segment_upper = segment.upper()\n        label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n\n        # Extract component/net names from codebook label (e.g., \"R152\", \"I2C_SDA\")\n        # Pattern: alphanumeric with underscores, typically in the label after \": \"\n        codebook_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', label)\n\n        score = 0.0\n        for comp in codebook_components:\n            if comp in segment_upper:\n                score += 0.3  # Strong boost for exact component match\n\n        return min(score, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_semantic_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate semantic matching using multiple signals\"\"\"\n        keyword_score = self.calculate_keyword_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        fuzzy_score = self.calculate_fuzzy_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        component_score = self.calculate_component_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n\n        # Weighted combination\n        # Keywords are most important, followed by component match, then fuzzy\n        total_score = (\n            keyword_score * 0.5 +\n            component_score * 0.3 +\n            fuzzy_score * 0.2\n        )\n\n        return total_score\n\n    def validate_station(self, station: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Validate if the codebook entry is applicable to the station\"\"\"\n        station_scope = codebook_entry.get('station_scope', '')\n        if not station_scope:\n            return True  # No restriction\n\n        allowed_stations = [s.strip() for s in station_scope.split(';')]\n        return station in allowed_stations\n\n    def find_best_match(\n        self,\n        segment: str,\n        product_id: str,\n        station: str\n    ) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.0, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n        best_entry = None\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Calculate match score\n            semantic_score = self.calculate_semantic_match_score(segment, entry)\n\n            # Validate station\n            station_valid = self.validate_station(station, entry)\n\n            # Apply station validation penalty\n            if not station_valid:\n                semantic_score *= 0.3  # Heavy penalty but not complete elimination\n\n            if semantic_score > best_score:\n                best_score = semantic_score\n                best_entry = entry\n                best_match = (semantic_score, station_valid)\n\n        # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.15  # Minimum threshold to accept a match\n\n        if best_score < CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD or best_entry is None:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                best_score * 0.5,  # Unknown predictions have lower confidence\n                f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f}, threshold: {CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD})\"\n            )\n\n        pred_code = best_entry['code']\n        pred_label = best_entry['standard_label']\n        station_valid = best_match[1]\n\n        # Calibrate confidence based on match quality and station validation\n        confidence = best_score\n        if not station_valid:\n            confidence *= 0.6  # Reduce confidence for station mismatch\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f}, WARNING: station mismatch)\"\n        else:\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f})\"\n\n        # Further reduce confidence if score is marginal\n        if confidence < 0.3:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                confidence * 0.5,\n                f\"Match confidence too low ({confidence:.3f})\"\n            )\n\n        return (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single test record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Preprocess text\n        preprocessed_text = self.preprocess_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        # Segment the text\n        segments = self.segment_text(preprocessed_text)\n\n        # Normalize each segment\n        normalized = []\n        for i, segment in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{i}\"\n\n            pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale = self.find_best_match(\n                segment, product_id, station\n            )\n\n            normalized.append({\n                \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": segment,\n                \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": round(confidence, 4),\n                \"rationale\": rationale\n            })\n\n        return {\n            \"record_id\": record_id,\n            \"product_id\": product_id,\n            \"station\": station,\n            \"engineer_id\": engineer_id,\n            \"raw_reason_text\": raw_reason_text,\n            \"normalized\": normalized\n        }\n\n    def normalize_all(self, input_csv: str, output_json: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all records and generate output JSON\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(input_csv, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                normalized_record = self.normalize_record(row)\n                records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        # Generate output\n        output = {\"records\": records}\n\n        with open(output_json, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Print statistics\n        total_segments = sum(len(r['normalized']) for r in records)\n        unknown_segments = sum(\n            1 for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n            if n['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN'\n        )\n\n        confidences = [\n            n['confidence']\n            for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n        ]\n\n        print(f\"Normalization Complete!\")\n        print(f\"Total records: {len(records)}\")\n        print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n        print(f\"Unknown segments: {unknown_segments} ({unknown_segments/total_segments*100:.1f}%)\")\n        print(f\"Average confidence: {sum(confidences)/len(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Min confidence: {min(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Max confidence: {max(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Output saved to: {output_json}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = DefectReasonNormalizer()\n    normalizer.normalize_all(\n        input_csv=\"/app/data/test_center_logs.csv\",\n        output_json=\"/app/output/solution.json\"\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":496,"cache_read_input_tokens":41509,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":496,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"76fbfc62-f418-4d68-bdab-8bccccf2a35c","uuid":"faa5437c-56b7-4e27-911e-e21a365ac721"}

Use SequenceMatcher for fuzzy matching\n        return SequenceMatcher(None, segment_lower, label_lower).ratio()\n\n    def calculate_component_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Boost score if component/net names match\"\"\"\n        segment_upper = segment.upper()\n        label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n\n        # Strong boost for exact component match\n\n        return min(score, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_semantic_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate semantic matching using multiple signals\"\"\"\n        keyword_score = self.calculate_keyword_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        fuzzy_score = self.calculate_fuzzy_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        component_score = self.calculate_component_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n\n        # Weighted combination\n        # Keywords are most important, followed by component match, then fuzzy\n        total_score = (\n            keyword_score * 0.5 +\n            component_score * 0.3 +\n            fuzzy_score * 0.2\n        )\n\n        return total_score\n\n    def validate_station(self, station: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Validate if the codebook entry is applicable to the station\"\"\"\n        station_scope = codebook_entry.get('station_scope', '')\n        if not station_scope:\n            return True  # No restriction\n\n        allowed_stations = [s.strip() for s in station_scope.split(';')]\n        return station in allowed_stations\n\n    def find_best_match(\n        self,\n        segment: str,\n        product_id: str,\n        station: str\n    ) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.0, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n        best_entry = None\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Calculate match score\n            semantic_score = self.calculate_semantic_match_score(segment, entry)\n\n            # Validate station\n            station_valid = self.validate_station(station, entry)\n\n            # Apply station validation penalty\n            if not station_valid:\n                semantic_score *= 0.3  # Heavy penalty but not complete elimination\n\n            if semantic_score > best_score:\n                best_score = semantic_score\n                best_entry = entry\n                best_match = (semantic_score, station_valid)\n\n        # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.15  # Minimum threshold to accept a match\n\n        if best_score < CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD or best_entry is None:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                best_score * 0.5,  # Unknown predictions have lower confidence\n                f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f}, threshold: {CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD})\"\n            )\n\n        pred_code = best_entry['code']\n        pred_label = best_entry['standard_label']\n        station_valid = best_match[1]\n\n        # Calibrate confidence based on match quality and station validation\n        confidence = best_score\n        if not station_valid:\n            confidence *= 0.6  # Reduce confidence for station mismatch\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f}, WARNING: station mismatch)\"\n        else:\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f})\"\n\n        # Further reduce confidence if score is marginal\n        if confidence < 0.3:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                confidence * 0.5,\n                f\"Match confidence too low ({confidence:.3f})\"\n            )\n\n        return (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single test record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Preprocess text\n        preprocessed_text = self.preprocess_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        # Segment the text\n        segments = self.segment_text(preprocessed_text)\n\n        # Normalize each segment\n        normalized = []\n        for i, segment in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{i}\"\n\n            pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale = self.find_best_match(\n                segment, product_id, station\n            )\n\n            normalized.append({\n                \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": segment,\n                \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": round(confidence, 4),\n                \"rationale\": rationale\n            })\n\n        return {\n            \"record_id\": record_id,\n            \"product_id\": product_id,\n            \"station\": station,\n            \"engineer_id\": engineer_id,\n            \"raw_reason_text\": raw_reason_text,\n            \"normalized\": normalized\n        }\n\n    def normalize_all(self, input_csv: str, output_json: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all records and generate output JSON\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(input_csv, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                normalized_record = self.normalize_record(row)\n                records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        # Generate output\n        output = {\"records\": records}\n\n        with open(output_json, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Print statistics\n        total_segments = sum(len(r['normalized']) for r in records)\n        unknown_segments = sum(\n            1 for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n            if n['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN'\n        )\n\n        confidences = [\n            n['confidence']\n            for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n        ]\n\n        print(f\"Normalization Complete!\")\n        print(f\"Total records: {len(records)}\")\n        print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n        print(f\"Unknown segments: {unknown_segments} ({unknown_segments/total_segments*100:.1f}%)\")\n        print(f\"Average confidence: {sum(confidences)/len(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Min confidence: {min(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Max confidence: {max(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Output saved to: {output_json}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = DefectReasonNormalizer()\n    normalizer.normalize_all(\n        input_csv=\"/app/data/test_center_logs.csv\",\n        output_json=\"/app/output/solution.json\"\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Split by common delimiters\n        keywords = re.split(r'[,，、;；/\\s]+', text_lower)\n        # Filter out very short keywords and common words\n        keywords = {k.strip() for k in keywords if len(k.strip()) > 1}\n        return keywords\n\n    def segment_text(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw defect reason into individual defect descriptions.\n        Handles multiple defects separated by delimiters like &, ; / etc.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return []\n\n        # Common separators: & ; + / 、and newlines\n        # But be careful not to split on / within descriptions like \"open/short\"\n        segments = []\n\n        # First split by strong delimiters\n        # Use pattern to split by & or ; or 、or multiple spaces with specific patterns\n        parts = re.split(r'\\s*[&;、]\\s*|\\s+(?:and|AND)\\s+', text)\n\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if part:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def calculate_keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate keyword-based matching score\"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = self.extract_keywords(segment)\n        codebook_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', ''))\n\n        if not segment_keywords or not codebook_keywords:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Calculate Jaccard similarity\n        intersection = segment_keywords & codebook_keywords\n        union = segment_keywords | codebook_keywords\n\n        if not union:\n            return 0.0\n\n        return len(intersection) / len(union)\n\n    def calculate_fuzzy_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate fuzzy string matching score using standard_label\"\"\"\n        segment_lower = segment.lower()\n        label_lower = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '').lower()\n\n        # Use SequenceMatcher for fuzzy matching\n        return SequenceMatcher(None, segment_lower, label_lower).ratio()\n\n    def calculate_component_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Boost score if component/net names match\"\"\"\n        segment_upper = segment.upper()\n        label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n\n        # Extract component/net names from codebook label (e.g., \"R152\", \"I2C_SDA\")\n        # Pattern: alphanumeric with underscores, typically in the label after \": \"\n        codebook_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', label)\n\n        score = 0.0\n        for comp in codebook_components:\n            if comp in segment_upper:\n                score += 0.3  # Strong boost for exact component match\n\n        return min(score, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_semantic_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate semantic matching using multiple signals\"\"\"\n        keyword_score = self.calculate_keyword_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        fuzzy_score = self.calculate_fuzzy_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        component_score = self.calculate_component_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n\n        # Weighted combination\n        # Keywords are most important, followed by component match, then fuzzy\n        total_score = (\n            keyword_score * 0.5 +\n            component_score * 0.3 +\n            fuzzy_score * 0.2\n        )\n\n        return total_score\n\n    def validate_station(self, station: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Validate if the codebook entry is applicable to the station\"\"\"\n        station_scope = codebook_entry.get('station_scope', '')\n        if not station_scope:\n            return True  # No restriction\n\n        allowed_stations = [s.strip() for s in station_scope.split(';')]\n        return station in allowed_stations\n\n    def find_best_match(\n        self,\n        segment: str,\n        product_id: str,\n        station: str\n    ) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.0, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n        best_entry = None\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Calculate match score\n            semantic_score = self.calculate_semantic_match_score(segment, entry)\n\n            # Validate station\n            station_valid = self.validate_station(station, entry)\n\n            # Apply station validation penalty\n            if not station_valid:\n                semantic_score *= 0.3  # Heavy penalty but not complete elimination\n\n            if semantic_score > best_score:\n                best_score = semantic_score\n                best_entry = entry\n                best_match = (semantic_score, station_valid)\n\n        # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.15  # Minimum threshold to accept a match\n\n        if best_score < CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD or best_entry is None:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                best_score * 0.5,  # Unknown predictions have lower confidence\n                f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f}, threshold: {CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD})\"\n            )\n\n        pred_code = best_entry['code']\n        pred_label = best_entry['standard_label']\n        station_valid = best_match[1]\n\n        # Calibrate confidence based on match quality and station validation\n        confidence = best_score\n        if not station_valid:\n            confidence *= 0.6  # Reduce confidence for station mismatch\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f}, WARNING: station mismatch)\"\n        else:\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f})\"\n\n        # Further reduce confidence if score is marginal\n        if confidence < 0.3:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                confidence * 0.5,\n                f\"Match confidence too low ({confidence:.3f})\"\n            )\n\n        return (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single test record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Preprocess text\n        preprocessed_text = self.preprocess_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        # Segment the text\n        segments = self.segment_text(preprocessed_text)\n\n        # Normalize each segment\n        normalized = []\n        for i, segment in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{i}\"\n\n            pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale = self.find_best_match(\n                segment, product_id, station\n            )\n\n            normalized.append({\n                \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": segment,\n                \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": round(confidence, 4),\n                \"rationale\": rationale\n            })\n\n        return {\n            \"record_id\": record_id,\n            \"product_id\": product_id,\n            \"station\": station,\n            \"engineer_id\": engineer_id,\n            \"raw_reason_text\": raw_reason_text,\n            \"normalized\": normalized\n        }\n\n    def normalize_all(self, input_csv: str, output_json: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all records and generate output JSON\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(input_csv, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                normalized_record = self.normalize_record(row)\n                records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        # Generate output\n        output = {\"records\": records}\n\n        with open(output_json, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Print statistics\n        total_segments = sum(len(r['normalized']) for r in records)\n        unknown_segments = sum(\n            1 for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n            if n['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN'\n        )\n\n        confidences = [\n            n['confidence']\n            for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n        ]\n\n        print(f\"Normalization Complete!\")\n        print(f\"Total records: {len(records)}\")\n        print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n        print(f\"Unknown segments: {unknown_segments} ({unknown_segments/total_segments*100:.1f}%)\")\n        print(f\"Average confidence: {sum(confidences)/len(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Min confidence: {min(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Max confidence: {max(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Output saved to: {output_json}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = DefectReasonNormalizer()\n    normalizer.normalize_all(\n        input_csv=\"/app/data/test_center_logs.csv\",\n        output_json=\"/app/output/solution.json\"\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":38,"oldLines":9,"newStart":38,"newLines":15,"lines":["         \"\"\"Extract keywords from text (both English and Chinese)\"\"\"","         text_lower = text.lower()","         # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.15  # Minimum threshold to accept a match\n\n        if best_score < CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD or best_entry is None:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                best_score * 0.5,  # Generate output\n        output = {\"records\": records}\n\n        with open(output_json, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Print statistics\n        total_segments = sum(len(r['normalized']) for r in records)\n        unknown_segments = sum(\n            1 for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n            if n['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN'\n        )\n\n        confidences = [\n            n['confidence']\n            for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n        ]\n\n        print(f\"Normalization Complete!\")\n        print(f\"Total records: {len(records)}\")\n        print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n        print(f\"Unknown segments: {unknown_segments} ({unknown_segments/total_segments*100:.1f}%)\")\n        print(f\"Average confidence: {sum(confidences)/len(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Min confidence: {min(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Max confidence: {max(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Output saved to: {output_json}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = DefectReasonNormalizer()\n    normalizer.normalize_all(\n        input_csv=\"/app/data/test_center_logs.csv\",\n        output_json=\"/app/output/solution.json\"\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":76,"oldLines":18,"newStart":76,"newLines":37,"lines":["         \"\"\"Calculate keyword-based matching score\"\"\"","         segment_keywords = self.extract_keywords(segment)","         codebook_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', ''))","+        label_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('standard_label', ''))"," ","-        if not segment_keywords or not codebook_keywords:","+        # Normalize whitespace\n        text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n        return text.strip()\n\n    def extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> set:\n        \"\"\"Extract keywords from text (both English and Chinese)\"\"\"\n        text_lower = text.lower()\n        # Split by common delimiters\n        keywords = re.split(r'[,，、;；\\s]+', text_lower)\n        # Filter out very short keywords and common words\n        keywords = {k.strip() for k in keywords if len(k.strip()) > 1}\n\n        # Also extract technical terms that might contain underscores or numbers\n        # e.g., I2C_SDA, R152, VDD_5V\n        tech_terms = re.findall(r'\\b[a-z0-9_]+\\b', text_lower)\n        keywords.update(tech_terms)\n\n        return keywords\n\n    def segment_text(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw defect reason into individual defect descriptions.\n        Handles multiple defects separated by delimiters like &, ; / etc.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return []\n\n        # Common separators: & ; + / 、and newlines\n        # But be careful not to split on / within descriptions like \"open/short\"\n        segments = []\n\n        # First split by strong delimiters\n        # Use pattern to split by & or ; or 、or multiple spaces with specific patterns\n        parts = re.split(r'\\s*[&;、]\\s*|\\s+(?:and|AND)\\s+', text)\n\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if part:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def calculate_keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate keyword-based matching score\"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = self.extract_keywords(segment)\n        codebook_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', ''))\n        label_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('standard_label', ''))\n\n        # Combine codebook keywords with label keywords\n        all_codebook_keywords = codebook_keywords | label_keywords\n\n        if not segment_keywords or not all_codebook_keywords:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Calculate weighted match\n        # Exact matches get full weight\n        exact_matches = segment_keywords & all_codebook_keywords\n\n        # Partial matches using substring matching\n        partial_match_count = 0\n        for seg_kw in segment_keywords:\n            if seg_kw not in exact_matches:\n                for cb_kw in all_codebook_keywords:\n                    # Check if either is substring of the other\n                    if len(seg_kw) >= 3 and len(cb_kw) >= 3:\n                        if seg_kw in cb_kw or cb_kw in seg_kw:\n                            partial_match_count += 0.5\n                            break\n\n        total_match = len(exact_matches) + partial_match_count\n        total_keywords = len(segment_keywords)\n\n        if total_keywords == 0:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Normalize by segment keywords (recall-based)\n        return min(total_match / total_keywords, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_fuzzy_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate fuzzy string matching score using standard_label\"\"\"\n        segment_lower = segment.lower()\n        label_lower = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '').lower()\n\n        # Use SequenceMatcher for fuzzy matching\n        return SequenceMatcher(None, segment_lower, label_lower).ratio()\n\n    def calculate_component_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Boost score if component/net names match\"\"\"\n        segment_upper = segment.upper()\n        label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n\n        # Extract component/net names from codebook label (e.g., \"R152\", \"I2C_SDA\")\n        # Pattern: alphanumeric with underscores, typically in the label after \": \"\n        codebook_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', label)\n\n        score = 0.0\n        for comp in codebook_components:\n            if comp in segment_upper:\n                score += 0.3  # Strong boost for exact component match\n\n        return min(score, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_semantic_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate semantic matching using multiple signals\"\"\"\n        keyword_score = self.calculate_keyword_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        fuzzy_score = self.calculate_fuzzy_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        component_score = self.calculate_component_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n\n        # Weighted combination\n        # Keywords are most important, followed by component match, then fuzzy\n        total_score = (\n            keyword_score * 0.5 +\n            component_score * 0.3 +\n            fuzzy_score * 0.2\n        )\n\n        return total_score\n\n    def validate_station(self, station: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Validate if the codebook entry is applicable to the station\"\"\"\n        station_scope = codebook_entry.get('station_scope', '')\n        if not station_scope:\n            return True  # No restriction\n\n        allowed_stations = [s.strip() for s in station_scope.split(';')]\n        return station in allowed_stations\n\n    def find_best_match(\n        self,\n        segment: str,\n        product_id: str,\n        station: str\n    ) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.0, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n        best_entry = None\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Calculate match score\n            semantic_score = self.calculate_semantic_match_score(segment, entry)\n\n            # Validate station\n            station_valid = self.validate_station(station, entry)\n\n            # Apply station validation penalty\n            if not station_valid:\n                semantic_score *= 0.3  # Heavy penalty but not complete elimination\n\n            if semantic_score > best_score:\n                best_score = semantic_score\n                best_entry = entry\n                best_match = (semantic_score, station_valid)\n\n        # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.15  # Minimum threshold to accept a match\n\n        if best_score < CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD or best_entry is None:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                best_score * 0.5,  # Unknown predictions have lower confidence\n                f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f}, threshold: {CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD})\"\n            )\n\n        pred_code = best_entry['code']\n        pred_label = best_entry['standard_label']\n        station_valid = best_match[1]\n\n        # Calibrate confidence based on match quality and station validation\n        confidence = best_score\n        if not station_valid:\n            confidence *= 0.6  # Reduce confidence for station mismatch\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f}, WARNING: station mismatch)\"\n        else:\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f})\"\n\n        # Further reduce confidence if score is marginal\n        if confidence < 0.3:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                confidence * 0.5,\n                f\"Match confidence too low ({confidence:.3f})\"\n            )\n\n        return (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single test record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Preprocess text\n        preprocessed_text = self.preprocess_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        # Segment the text\n        segments = self.segment_text(preprocessed_text)\n\n        # Normalize each segment\n        normalized = []\n        for i, segment in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{i}\"\n\n            pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale = self.find_best_match(\n                segment, product_id, station\n            )\n\n            normalized.append({\n                \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": segment,\n                \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": round(confidence, 4),\n                \"rationale\": rationale\n            })\n\n        return {\n            \"record_id\": record_id,\n            \"product_id\": product_id,\n            \"station\": station,\n            \"engineer_id\": engineer_id,\n            \"raw_reason_text\": raw_reason_text,\n            \"normalized\": normalized\n        }\n\n    def normalize_all(self, input_csv: str, output_json: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all records and generate output JSON\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(input_csv, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                normalized_record = self.normalize_record(row)\n                records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        # Generate output\n        output = {\"records\": records}\n\n        with open(output_json, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Print statistics\n        total_segments = sum(len(r['normalized']) for r in records)\n        unknown_segments = sum(\n            1 for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n            if n['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN'\n        )\n\n        confidences = [\n            n['confidence']\n            for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n        ]\n\n        print(f\"Normalization Complete!\")\n        print(f\"Total records: {len(records)}\")\n        print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n        print(f\"Unknown segments: {unknown_segments} ({unknown_segments/total_segments*100:.1f}%)\")\n        print(f\"Average confidence: {sum(confidences)/len(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Min confidence: {min(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Max confidence: {max(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Output saved to: {output_json}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = DefectReasonNormalizer()\n    normalizer.normalize_all(\n        input_csv=\"/app/data/test_center_logs.csv\",\n        output_json=\"/app/output/solution.json\"\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":120,"oldLines":16,"newStart":120,"newLines":25,"lines":["         \"\"\"Boost score if component/net names match\"\"\"","         segment_upper = segment.upper()","         label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')","+        keywords = codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', '')"," ","-        # Weighted combination\n        # Component match is very important, followed by keywords, then fuzzy\n        # If there's a strong component match, boost significantly\n        if component_score > 0.3:\n            total_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.35 +\n                component_score * 0.50 +\n                fuzzy_score * 0.15\n            )\n        else:\n            total_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.60 +\n                component_score * 0.25 +\n                fuzzy_score * 0.15\n            )\n\n        return total_score","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nManufacturing Test Center Defect Reason Normalization\nNormalizes hand-written defect reasons to standardized codebook entries\n\"\"\"\n\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport re\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Tuple\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\n\n\nclass DefectReasonNormalizer:\n    def __init__(self, codebook_dir=\"/app/data\"):\n        self.codebook_dir = codebook_dir\n        self.codebooks = {}\n        self.load_codebooks()\n\n    def load_codebooks(self):\n        \"\"\"Load all product codebooks\"\"\"\n        products = [\"P1_POWER\", \"P2_CTRL\", \"P3_RF\"]\n        for product in products:\n            codebook_path = f\"{self.codebook_dir}/codebook_{product}.csv\"\n            with open(codebook_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n                reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n                self.codebooks[product] = list(reader)\n\n    def preprocess_text(self, text: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize text for matching - remove extra spaces, normalize case\"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return \"\"\n        # Normalize whitespace\n        text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n        return text.strip()\n\n    def extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> set:\n        \"\"\"Extract keywords from text (both English and Chinese)\"\"\"\n        text_lower = text.lower()\n        # Split by common delimiters\n        keywords = re.split(r'[,，、;；\\s]+', text_lower)\n        # Filter out very short keywords and common words\n        keywords = {k.strip() for k in keywords if len(k.strip()) > 1}\n\n        # Also extract technical terms that might contain underscores or numbers\n        # e.g., I2C_SDA, R152, VDD_5V\n        tech_terms = re.findall(r'\\b[a-z0-9_]+\\b', text_lower)\n        keywords.update(tech_terms)\n\n        return keywords\n\n    def segment_text(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw defect reason into individual defect descriptions.\n        Handles multiple defects separated by delimiters like &, ; / etc.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return []\n\n        # Common separators: & ; + / 、and newlines\n        # But be careful not to split on / within descriptions like \"open/short\"\n        segments = []\n\n        # First split by strong delimiters\n        # Use pattern to split by & or ; or 、or multiple spaces with specific patterns\n        parts = re.split(r'\\s*[&;、]\\s*|\\s+(?:and|AND)\\s+', text)\n\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if part:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def calculate_keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate keyword-based matching score\"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = self.extract_keywords(segment)\n        codebook_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', ''))\n        label_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('standard_label', ''))\n\n        # Combine codebook keywords with label keywords\n        all_codebook_keywords = codebook_keywords | label_keywords\n\n        if not segment_keywords or not all_codebook_keywords:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Calculate weighted match\n        # Exact matches get full weight\n        exact_matches = segment_keywords & all_codebook_keywords\n\n        # Partial matches using substring matching\n        partial_match_count = 0\n        for seg_kw in segment_keywords:\n            if seg_kw not in exact_matches:\n                for cb_kw in all_codebook_keywords:\n                    # Check if either is substring of the other\n                    if len(seg_kw) >= 3 and len(cb_kw) >= 3:\n                        if seg_kw in cb_kw or cb_kw in seg_kw:\n                            partial_match_count += 0.5\n                            break\n\n        total_match = len(exact_matches) + partial_match_count\n        total_keywords = len(segment_keywords)\n\n        if total_keywords == 0:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Normalize by segment keywords (recall-based)\n        return min(total_match / total_keywords, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_fuzzy_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate fuzzy string matching score using standard_label\"\"\"\n        segment_lower = segment.lower()\n        label_lower = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '').lower()\n\n        # Use SequenceMatcher for fuzzy matching\n        return SequenceMatcher(None, segment_lower, label_lower).ratio()\n\n    def calculate_component_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Boost score if component/net names match\"\"\"\n        segment_upper = segment.upper()\n        label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n        keywords = codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', '')\n\n        # Extract component/net names from both label and keywords\n        codebook_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', label)\n        keyword_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', keywords.upper())\n        all_components = set(codebook_components + keyword_components)\n\n        score = 0.0\n        for comp in all_components:\n            if comp in segment_upper:\n                score += 0.4  # Strong boost for exact component match\n\n        # Also check for partial component matches (e.g., \"R152\" in \"R152_NET\")\n        segment_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', segment_upper)\n        for seg_comp in segment_components:\n            for cb_comp in all_components:\n                if seg_comp != cb_comp and (seg_comp in cb_comp or cb_comp in seg_comp):\n                    score += 0.2\n\n        return min(score, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_semantic_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate semantic matching using multiple signals\"\"\"\n        keyword_score = self.calculate_keyword_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        fuzzy_score = self.calculate_fuzzy_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        component_score = self.calculate_component_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n\n        # Weighted combination\n        # Keywords are most important, followed by component match, then fuzzy\n        total_score = (\n            keyword_score * 0.5 +\n            component_score * 0.3 +\n            fuzzy_score * 0.2\n        )\n\n        return total_score\n\n    def validate_station(self, station: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Validate if the codebook entry is applicable to the station\"\"\"\n        station_scope = codebook_entry.get('station_scope', '')\n        if not station_scope:\n            return True  # No restriction\n\n        allowed_stations = [s.strip() for s in station_scope.split(';')]\n        return station in allowed_stations\n\n    def find_best_match(\n        self,\n        segment: str,\n        product_id: str,\n        station: str\n    ) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.0, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n        best_entry = None\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Calculate match score\n            semantic_score = self.calculate_semantic_match_score(segment, entry)\n\n            # Validate station\n            station_valid = self.validate_station(station, entry)\n\n            # Apply station validation penalty\n            if not station_valid:\n                semantic_score *= 0.3  # Heavy penalty but not complete elimination\n\n            if semantic_score > best_score:\n                best_score = semantic_score\n                best_entry = entry\n                best_match = (semantic_score, station_valid)\n\n        # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.15  # Minimum threshold to accept a match\n\n        if best_score < CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD or best_entry is None:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                best_score * 0.5,  # Unknown predictions have lower confidence\n                f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f}, threshold: {CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD})\"\n            )\n\n        pred_code = best_entry['code']\n        pred_label = best_entry['standard_label']\n        station_valid = best_match[1]\n\n        # Calibrate confidence based on match quality and station validation\n        confidence = best_score\n        if not station_valid:\n            confidence *= 0.6  # Reduce confidence for station mismatch\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f}, WARNING: station mismatch)\"\n        else:\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f})\"\n\n        # Further reduce confidence if score is marginal\n        if confidence < 0.3:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                confidence * 0.5,\n                f\"Match confidence too low ({confidence:.3f})\"\n            )\n\n        return (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single test record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Preprocess text\n        preprocessed_text = self.preprocess_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        # Segment the text\n        segments = self.segment_text(preprocessed_text)\n\n        # Normalize each segment\n        normalized = []\n        for i, segment in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{i}\"\n\n            pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale = self.find_best_match(\n                segment, product_id, station\n            )\n\n            normalized.append({\n                \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": segment,\n                \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": round(confidence, 4),\n                \"rationale\": rationale\n            })\n\n        return {\n            \"record_id\": record_id,\n            \"product_id\": product_id,\n            \"station\": station,\n            \"engineer_id\": engineer_id,\n            \"raw_reason_text\": raw_reason_text,\n            \"normalized\": normalized\n        }\n\n    def normalize_all(self, input_csv: str, output_json: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all records and generate output JSON\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(input_csv, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                normalized_record = self.normalize_record(row)\n                records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        # Generate output\n        output = {\"records\": records}\n\n        with open(output_json, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Print statistics\n        total_segments = sum(len(r['normalized']) for r in records)\n        unknown_segments = sum(\n            1 for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n            if n['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN'\n        )\n\n        confidences = [\n            n['confidence']\n            for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n        ]\n\n        print(f\"Normalization Complete!\")\n        print(f\"Total records: {len(records)}\")\n        print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n        print(f\"Unknown segments: {unknown_segments} ({unknown_segments/total_segments*100:.1f}%)\")\n        print(f\"Average confidence: {sum(confidences)/len(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Min confidence: {min(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Max confidence: {max(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Output saved to: {output_json}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = DefectReasonNormalizer()\n    normalizer.normalize_all(\n        input_csv=\"/app/data/test_center_logs.csv\",\n        output_json=\"/app/output/solution.json\"\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":148,"oldLines":12,"newStart":148,"newLines":20,"lines":["         component_score = self.calculate_component_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)"," ","         # Normalize whitespace\n        text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n        return text.strip()\n\n    def extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> set:\n        \"\"\"Extract keywords from text (both English and Chinese)\"\"\"\n        text_lower = text.lower()\n        # Split by common delimiters\n        keywords = re.split(r'[,，、;；\\s]+', text_lower)\n        # Filter out very short keywords and common words\n        keywords = {k.strip() for k in keywords if len(k.strip()) > 1}\n\n        # Also extract technical terms that might contain underscores or numbers\n        # e.g., I2C_SDA, R152, VDD_5V\n        tech_terms = re.findall(r'\\b[a-z0-9_]+\\b', text_lower)\n        keywords.update(tech_terms)\n\n        return keywords\n\n    def segment_text(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw defect reason into individual defect descriptions.\n        Handles multiple defects separated by delimiters like &, ; / etc.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return []\n\n        # Common separators: & ; + / 、and newlines\n        # But be careful not to split on / within descriptions like \"open/short\"\n        segments = []\n\n        # First split by strong delimiters\n        # Use pattern to split by & or ; or 、or multiple spaces with specific patterns\n        parts = re.split(r'\\s*[&;、]\\s*|\\s+(?:and|AND)\\s+', text)\n\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if part:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def calculate_keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate keyword-based matching score\"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = self.extract_keywords(segment)\n        codebook_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', ''))\n        label_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('standard_label', ''))\n\n        # Combine codebook keywords with label keywords\n        all_codebook_keywords = codebook_keywords | label_keywords\n\n        if not segment_keywords or not all_codebook_keywords:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Calculate weighted match\n        # Exact matches get full weight\n        exact_matches = segment_keywords & all_codebook_keywords\n\n        # Partial matches using substring matching\n        partial_match_count = 0\n        for seg_kw in segment_keywords:\n            if seg_kw not in exact_matches:\n                for cb_kw in all_codebook_keywords:\n                    # Check if either is substring of the other\n                    if len(seg_kw) >= 3 and len(cb_kw) >= 3:\n                        if seg_kw in cb_kw or cb_kw in seg_kw:\n                            partial_match_count += 0.5\n                            break\n\n        total_match = len(exact_matches) + partial_match_count\n        total_keywords = len(segment_keywords)\n\n        if total_keywords == 0:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Normalize by segment keywords (recall-based)\n        return min(total_match / total_keywords, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_fuzzy_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate fuzzy string matching score using standard_label\"\"\"\n        segment_lower = segment.lower()\n        label_lower = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '').lower()\n\n        # Use SequenceMatcher for fuzzy matching\n        return SequenceMatcher(None, segment_lower, label_lower).ratio()\n\n    def calculate_component_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Boost score if component/net names match\"\"\"\n        segment_upper = segment.upper()\n        label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n        keywords = codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', '')\n\n        # Extract component/net names from both label and keywords\n        codebook_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', label)\n        keyword_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', keywords.upper())\n        all_components = set(codebook_components + keyword_components)\n\n        score = 0.0\n        for comp in all_components:\n            if comp in segment_upper:\n                score += 0.4  # Strong boost for exact component match\n\n        # Also check for partial component matches (e.g., \"R152\" in \"R152_NET\")\n        segment_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', segment_upper)\n        for seg_comp in segment_components:\n            for cb_comp in all_components:\n                if seg_comp != cb_comp and (seg_comp in cb_comp or cb_comp in seg_comp):\n                    score += 0.2\n\n        return min(score, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_semantic_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate semantic matching using multiple signals\"\"\"\n        keyword_score = self.calculate_keyword_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        fuzzy_score = self.calculate_fuzzy_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        component_score = self.calculate_component_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n\n        # Weighted combination\n        # Component match is very important, followed by keywords, then fuzzy\n        # If there's a strong component match, boost significantly\n        if component_score > 0.3:\n            total_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.35 +\n                component_score * 0.50 +\n                fuzzy_score * 0.15\n            )\n        else:\n            total_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.60 +\n                component_score * 0.25 +\n                fuzzy_score * 0.15\n            )\n\n        return total_score\n\n    def validate_station(self, station: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Validate if the codebook entry is applicable to the station\"\"\"\n        station_scope = codebook_entry.get('station_scope', '')\n        if not station_scope:\n            return True  # No restriction\n\n        allowed_stations = [s.strip() for s in station_scope.split(';')]\n        return station in allowed_stations\n\n    def find_best_match(\n        self,\n        segment: str,\n        product_id: str,\n        station: str\n    ) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.0, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n        best_entry = None\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Calculate match score\n            semantic_score = self.calculate_semantic_match_score(segment, entry)\n\n            # Validate station\n            station_valid = self.validate_station(station, entry)\n\n            # Apply station validation penalty\n            if not station_valid:\n                semantic_score *= 0.3  # Heavy penalty but not complete elimination\n\n            if semantic_score > best_score:\n                best_score = semantic_score\n                best_entry = entry\n                best_match = (semantic_score, station_valid)\n\n        # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.15  # Minimum threshold to accept a match\n\n        if best_score < CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD or best_entry is None:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                best_score * 0.5,  # Unknown predictions have lower confidence\n                f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f}, threshold: {CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD})\"\n            )\n\n        pred_code = best_entry['code']\n        pred_label = best_entry['standard_label']\n        station_valid = best_match[1]\n\n        # Calibrate confidence based on match quality and station validation\n        confidence = best_score\n        if not station_valid:\n            confidence *= 0.6  # Reduce confidence for station mismatch\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f}, WARNING: station mismatch)\"\n        else:\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f})\"\n\n        # Further reduce confidence if score is marginal\n        if confidence < 0.3:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                confidence * 0.5,\n                f\"Match confidence too low ({confidence:.3f})\"\n            )\n\n        return (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n\n    def normalize_record(self, record: Dict) -> Dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a single test record\"\"\"\n        record_id = record['record_id']\n        product_id = record['product_id']\n        station = record['station']\n        engineer_id = record['engineer_id']\n        raw_reason_text = record['raw_reason_text']\n\n        # Preprocess text\n        preprocessed_text = self.preprocess_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        # Segment the text\n        segments = self.segment_text(preprocessed_text)\n\n        # Normalize each segment\n        normalized = []\n        for i, segment in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{i}\"\n\n            pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale = self.find_best_match(\n                segment, product_id, station\n            )\n\n            normalized.append({\n                \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": segment,\n                \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": round(confidence, 4),\n                \"rationale\": rationale\n            })\n\n        return {\n            \"record_id\": record_id,\n            \"product_id\": product_id,\n            \"station\": station,\n            \"engineer_id\": engineer_id,\n            \"raw_reason_text\": raw_reason_text,\n            \"normalized\": normalized\n        }\n\n    def normalize_all(self, input_csv: str, output_json: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all records and generate output JSON\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(input_csv, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                normalized_record = self.normalize_record(row)\n                records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        # Generate output\n        output = {\"records\": records}\n\n        with open(output_json, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(output, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Print statistics\n        total_segments = sum(len(r['normalized']) for r in records)\n        unknown_segments = sum(\n            1 for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n            if n['pred_code'] == 'UNKNOWN'\n        )\n\n        confidences = [\n            n['confidence']\n            for r in records\n            for n in r['normalized']\n        ]\n\n        print(f\"Normalization Complete!\")\n        print(f\"Total records: {len(records)}\")\n        print(f\"Total segments: {total_segments}\")\n        print(f\"Unknown segments: {unknown_segments} ({unknown_segments/total_segments*100:.1f}%)\")\n        print(f\"Average confidence: {sum(confidences)/len(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Min confidence: {min(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Max confidence: {max(confidences):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"Output saved to: {output_json}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    normalizer = DefectReasonNormalizer()\n    normalizer.normalize_all(\n        input_csv=\"/app/data/test_center_logs.csv\",\n        output_json=\"/app/output/solution.json\"\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":209,"oldLines":13,"newStart":209,"newLines":13,"lines":["                 best_match = (semantic_score, station_valid)"," ","         # re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text)\n        return text.strip()\n\n    def extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> set:\n        \"\"\"Extract keywords from text (both English and Chinese)\"\"\"\n        text_lower = text.lower()\n        # Split by common delimiters\n        keywords = re.split(r'[,，、;；\\s]+', text_lower)\n        # Filter out very short keywords and common words\n        keywords = {k.strip() for k in keywords if len(k.strip()) > 1}\n\n        # Also extract technical terms that might contain underscores or numbers\n        # e.g., I2C_SDA, R152, VDD_5V\n        tech_terms = re.findall(r'\\b[a-z0-9_]+\\b', text_lower)\n        keywords.update(tech_terms)\n\n        return keywords\n\n    def segment_text(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw defect reason into individual defect descriptions.\n        Handles multiple defects separated by delimiters like &, ; / etc.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return []\n\n        # Common separators: & ; + / 、and newlines\n        # But be careful not to split on / within descriptions like \"open/short\"\n        segments = []\n\n        # First split by strong delimiters\n        # Use pattern to split by & or ; or 、or multiple spaces with specific patterns\n        parts = re.split(r'\\s*[&;、]\\s*|\\s+(?:and|AND)\\s+', text)\n\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if part:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def calculate_keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate keyword-based matching score\"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = self.extract_keywords(segment)\n        codebook_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', ''))\n        label_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('standard_label', ''))\n\n        # Combine codebook keywords with label keywords\n        all_codebook_keywords = codebook_keywords | label_keywords\n\n        if not segment_keywords or not all_codebook_keywords:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Calculate weighted match\n        # Exact matches get full weight\n        exact_matches = segment_keywords & all_codebook_keywords\n\n        # Partial matches using substring matching\n        partial_match_count = 0\n        for seg_kw in segment_keywords:\n            if seg_kw not in exact_matches:\n                for cb_kw in all_codebook_keywords:\n                    # Check if either is substring of the other\n                    if len(seg_kw) >= 3 and len(cb_kw) >= 3:\n                        if seg_kw in cb_kw or cb_kw in seg_kw:\n                            partial_match_count += 0.5\n                            break\n\n        total_match = len(exact_matches) + partial_match_count\n        total_keywords = len(segment_keywords)\n\n        if total_keywords == 0:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Normalize by segment keywords (recall-based)\n        return min(total_match / total_keywords, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_fuzzy_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate fuzzy string matching score using standard_label\"\"\"\n        segment_lower = segment.lower()\n        label_lower = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '').lower()\n\n        # Use SequenceMatcher for fuzzy matching\n        return SequenceMatcher(None, segment_lower, label_lower).ratio()\n\n    def calculate_component_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Boost score if component/net names match\"\"\"\n        segment_upper = segment.upper()\n        label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n        keywords = codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', '')\n\n        # Extract component/net names from both label and keywords\n        codebook_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', label)\n        keyword_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', keywords.upper())\n        all_components = set(codebook_components + keyword_components)\n\n        score = 0.0\n        for comp in all_components:\n            if comp in segment_upper:\n                score += 0.4  # Strong boost for exact component match\n\n        # Also check for partial component matches (e.g., \"R152\" in \"R152_NET\")\n        segment_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', segment_upper)\n        for seg_comp in segment_components:\n            for cb_comp in all_components:\n                if seg_comp != cb_comp and (seg_comp in cb_comp or cb_comp in seg_comp):\n                    score += 0.2\n\n        return min(score, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_semantic_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate semantic matching using multiple signals\"\"\"\n        keyword_score = self.calculate_keyword_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        fuzzy_score = self.calculate_fuzzy_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        component_score = self.calculate_component_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n\n        # Weighted combination\n        # Component match is very important, followed by keywords, then fuzzy\n        # If there's a strong component match, boost significantly\n        if component_score > 0.3:\n            total_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.35 +\n                component_score * 0.50 +\n                fuzzy_score * 0.15\n            )\n        else:\n            total_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.60 +\n                component_score * 0.25 +\n                fuzzy_score * 0.15\n            )\n\n        return total_score\n\n    def validate_station(self, station: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Validate if the codebook entry is applicable to the station\"\"\"\n        station_scope = codebook_entry.get('station_scope', '')\n        if not station_scope:\n            return True  # No restriction\n\n        allowed_stations = [s.strip() for s in station_scope.split(';')]\n        return station in allowed_stations\n\n    def find_best_match(\n        self,\n        segment: str,\n        product_id: str,\n        station: str\n    ) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.0, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n        best_entry = None\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Calculate match score\n            semantic_score = self.calculate_semantic_match_score(segment, entry)\n\n            # Validate station\n            station_valid = self.validate_station(station, entry)\n\n            # Apply station validation penalty\n            if not station_valid:\n                semantic_score *= 0.3  # Heavy penalty but not complete elimination\n\n            if semantic_score > best_score:\n                best_score = semantic_score\n                best_entry = entry\n                best_match = (semantic_score, station_valid)\n\n        # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.20  # Minimum threshold to accept a match\n\n        if best_score < CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD or best_entry is None:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                best_score * 0.4,  # Unknown predictions have lower confidence\n                f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f}, threshold: {CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD})\"\n            )\n\n        pred_code = best_entry['code']\n        pred_label = best_entry['standard_label']\n        station_valid = best_match[1]\n\n        # Calibrate confidence based on match quality and station validation\n        confidence = best_score\n\n        # Apply sigmoid-like transformation to spread confidence values\n        # This helps differentiate between good and great matches\n        confidence = 1 / (1 + (0.5 / confidence)**2)\n\n        if not station_valid:\n            confidence *= 0.7  # Reduce confidence for station mismatch\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f}, WARNING: station mismatch)\"\n        else:\n            rationale = f\"Matched to '{pred_label}' (score: {best_score:.3f})\"\n\n \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": segment,\n                \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": Filter out very short keywords and common words\n        keywords = {k.strip() for k in keywords if len(k.strip()) > 1}\n\n        # Also extract technical terms that might contain underscores or numbers\n        # e.g., I2C_SDA, R152, VDD_5V\n        tech_terms = re.findall(r'\\b[a-z0-9_]+\\b', text_lower)\n        keywords.update(tech_terms)\n\n        return keywords\n\n    def segment_text(self, text: str) -> List[str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Segment raw defect reason into individual defect descriptions.\n        Handles multiple defects separated by delimiters like &, ; / etc.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not text:\n            return []\n\n        # Common separators: & ; + / 、and newlines\n        # But be careful not to split on / within descriptions like \"open/short\"\n        segments = []\n\n        # First split by strong delimiters\n        # Use pattern to split by & or ; or 、or multiple spaces with specific patterns\n        parts = re.split(r'\\s*[&;、]\\s*|\\s+(?:and|AND)\\s+', text)\n\n        for part in parts:\n            part = part.strip()\n            if part:\n                segments.append(part)\n\n        return segments if segments else [text]\n\n    def calculate_keyword_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate keyword-based matching score\"\"\"\n        segment_keywords = self.extract_keywords(segment)\n        codebook_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', ''))\n        label_keywords = self.extract_keywords(codebook_entry.get('standard_label', ''))\n\n        # Combine codebook keywords with label keywords\n        all_codebook_keywords = codebook_keywords | label_keywords\n\n        if not segment_keywords or not all_codebook_keywords:\n            return 0.0\n\n        # Calculate weighted match\n        # Exact matches get full weight\n        exact_matches = segment_keywords & all_codebook_keywords\n\n        # Partial matches using substring matching\n        partial_match_count = 0\n        for seg_kw in segment_keywords:\n            if seg_kw not in exact_matches:\n                for cb_kw in all_codebook_keywords:\n                    #
