Use SequenceMatcher for fuzzy matching\n        return SequenceMatcher(None, segment_lower, label_lower).ratio()\n\n    def calculate_component_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Boost score if component/net names match\"\"\"\n        segment_upper = segment.upper()\n        label = codebook_entry.get('standard_label', '')\n        keywords = codebook_entry.get('keywords_examples', '')\n\n        # Extract component/net names from both label and keywords\n        codebook_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', label)\n        keyword_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', keywords.upper())\n        all_components = set(codebook_components + keyword_components)\n\n        score = 0.0\n        for comp in all_components:\n            if comp in segment_upper:\n                score += 0.4  # Strong boost for exact component match\n\n        # Also check for partial component matches (e.g., \"R152\" in \"R152_NET\")\n        segment_components = re.findall(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]+\\b', segment_upper)\n        for seg_comp in segment_components:\n            for cb_comp in all_components:\n                if seg_comp != cb_comp and (seg_comp in cb_comp or cb_comp in seg_comp):\n                    score += 0.2\n\n        return min(score, 1.0)\n\n    def calculate_semantic_match_score(self, segment: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calculate semantic matching using multiple signals\"\"\"\n        keyword_score = self.calculate_keyword_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        fuzzy_score = self.calculate_fuzzy_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n        component_score = self.calculate_component_match_score(segment, codebook_entry)\n\n        # Weighted combination\n        # Component match is very important, followed by keywords, then fuzzy\n        # If there's a strong component match, boost significantly\n        if component_score > 0.3:\n            total_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.35 +\n                component_score * 0.50 +\n                fuzzy_score * 0.15\n            )\n        else:\n            total_score = (\n                keyword_score * 0.60 +\n                component_score * 0.25 +\n                fuzzy_score * 0.15\n            )\n\n        return total_score\n\n    def validate_station(self, station: str, codebook_entry: Dict) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Validate if the codebook entry is applicable to the station\"\"\"\n        station_scope = codebook_entry.get('station_scope', '')\n        if not station_scope:\n            return True  # No restriction\n\n        allowed_stations = [s.strip() for s in station_scope.split(';')]\n        return station in allowed_stations\n\n    def find_best_match(\n        self,\n        segment: str,\n        product_id: str,\n        station: str\n    ) -> Tuple[str, str, float, str]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\n        Returns: (pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale)\n        \"\"\"\n        if product_id not in self.codebooks:\n            return (\"UNKNOWN\", \"\", 0.0, f\"Product {product_id} not found in codebooks\")\n\n        codebook = self.codebooks[product_id]\n        best_match = None\n        best_score = 0.0\n        best_entry = None\n\n        for entry in codebook:\n            # Calculate match score\n            semantic_score = self.calculate_semantic_match_score(segment, entry)\n\n            # Validate station\n            station_valid = self.validate_station(station, entry)\n\n            # Apply station validation penalty\n            if not station_valid:\n                semantic_score *= 0.3  # Heavy penalty but not complete elimination\n\n            if semantic_score > best_score:\n                best_score = semantic_score\n                best_entry = entry\n                best_match = (semantic_score, station_valid)\n\n        # Determine if match is good enough\n        CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.20  # Minimum threshold to accept a match\n\n        if best_score < CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD or best_entry is None:\n            return (\n                \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"\",\n                best_score * 0.4,  # Unknown predictions have lower confidence\n                f\"No confident match found (best score: {best_score:.3f}, threshold: {CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD})\"\n            )\n\n        pred_code = best_entry['code']\n        pred_label = best_entry['standard_label']\n        station_valid = best_match[1]\n\n        # Calibrate confidence based on match quality and station validation\n        confidence = best_score\n\n        # Apply sigmoid-like transformation to spread confidence values\n        # This helps differentiate between good and great matches\n        confidence = 1 / (1 + (0.5 / confidence)**2)\n\n        if not station_valid:\n            confidence *= 0.7  # Preprocess text\n        preprocessed_text = self.preprocess_text(raw_reason_text)\n\n        # Segment the text\n        segments = self.segment_text(preprocessed_text)\n\n        # Normalize each segment\n        normalized = []\n        for i, segment in enumerate(segments, 1):\n            segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{i}\"\n\n            pred_code, pred_label, confidence, rationale = self.find_best_match(\n                segment, product_id, station\n            )\n\n            normalized.append({\n                \"segment_id\": segment_id,\n                \"span_text\": segment,\n                \"pred_code\": pred_code,\n                \"pred_label\": pred_label,\n                \"confidence\": round(confidence, 4),\n                \"rationale\": rationale\n            })\n\n        return {\n            \"record_id\": record_id,\n            \"product_id\": product_id,\n            \"station\": station,\n            \"engineer_id\": engineer_id,\n            \"raw_reason_text\": raw_reason_text,\n            \"normalized\": normalized\n        }\n\n    def normalize_all(self, input_csv: str, output_json: str):\n        \"\"\"Process all records and generate output JSON\"\"\"\n        records = []\n\n        with open(input_csv, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n            for row in reader:\n                normalized_record = self.normalize_record(row)\n                records.append(normalized_record)\n\n        #
